    metadata:
      author: supabase
      version: "0.1.2"
    ---
    
    # Supabase
    
    ## Core Principles
    
    **1. Supabase changes frequently — verify against changelog and current docs before implementing.**
    Do not rely on training data for Supabase features. Function signatures, config.toml settings, and API conventions change between versions.
    
    First, fetch `https://supabase.com/changelog.md` (a lightweight summary index — not a heavy pull), scan for `breaking-change` tags relevant to your task, and follow the linked page for any that apply. Then look up the relevant topic using the documentation access methods below.
    
    **2. Verify your work.**
    After implementing any fix, run a test query to confirm the change works. A fix without verification is incomplete.
    
    **3. Recover from errors, don't loop.**
    If an approach fails after 2-3 attempts, stop and reconsider. Try a different method, check documentation, inspect the error more carefully, and review relevant logs when available. Supabase issues are not always solved by retrying the same command, and the answer is not always in the logs, but logs are often worth checking before proceeding.
    
    **4. Exposing tables to the Data API:** Depending on the user's [Data API settings](https://supabase.com/dashboard/project/<ref>/integrations/data_api/settings), newly created tables may not be automatically exposed via the Data (REST) API. If this is the case, `anon` and `authenticated` roles will need to be explicitly granted access.
    
    > Note that this is separate from RLS, which controls which _rows_ are visible once a table is accessible, not whether the table is accessible at all.
    
    When a user reports a SQL-created table is unexpectedly inaccessible, check their Data API settings and whether the roles have been granted access via explicit `GRANT` SQL. When granting public (`anon`/`authenticated`) access, always enable RLS too. See [Exposing a Table to the Data API](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/api/securing-your-api.md) for the full setup workflow.
    
    **5. RLS in exposed schemas.**
    Enable RLS on every table in any exposed schema, which includes `public` by default. This is critical in Supabase because tables in exposed schemas can be reachable through the Data API when the `anon`/`authenticated` roles have access (see [Exposing a Table to the Data API](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/api/securing-your-api.md)). For private schemas, prefer RLS as defense in depth. After enabling RLS, create policies that match the actual access model rather than defaulting every table to the same `auth.uid()` pattern.
    
    **6. Security checklist.**
    When working on any Supabase task that touches auth, RLS, views, storage, or user data, run through this checklist. These are Supabase-specific security traps that silently create vulnerabilities:
    
    - **Auth and session security**
      - **Never use `user_metadata` claims in JWT-based authorization decisions.** In Supabase, `raw_user_meta_data` is user-editable and can appear in `auth.jwt()`, so it is unsafe for RLS policies or any other authorization logic. Store authorization data in `raw_app_meta_data` / `app_metadata` instead.
      - **Deleting a user does not invalidate existing access tokens.** Sign out or revoke sessions first, keep JWT expiry short for sensitive apps, and for strict guarantees validate `session_id` against `auth.sessions` on sensitive operations.
      - **If you use `app_metadata` or `auth.jwt()` for authorization, remember JWT claims are not always fresh until the user's token is refreshed.**
    
    - **API key and client exposure**
      - **Never expose the `service_role` or secret key in public clients.** Prefer publishable keys for frontend code. Legacy `anon` keys are only for compatibility. In Next.js, any `NEXT_PUBLIC_` env var is sent to the browser.
      - **Use scoped personal access tokens for the Management API, CLI, and MCP server.** A classic personal access token carries the user's full account access, on every organization and project. For scripts, CI, and agents, have the user create a scoped token limited to the organizations, projects, and permissions the task needs. The [personal access tokens guide](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/personal-access-tokens.md) lists the permission each Management API endpoint and MCP tool requires. Look permissions up there rather than guessing.
    
    - **RLS, views, and privileged database code**
      - **Views bypass RLS by default.** In Postgres 15 and above, use `CREATE VIEW ... WITH (security_invoker = true)`. In older versions of Postgres, protect your views by revoking access from the `anon` and `authenticated` roles, or by putting them in an unexposed schema.
      - **UPDATE requires a SELECT policy.** In Postgres RLS, an UPDATE needs to first SELECT the row. Without a SELECT policy, updates silently return 0 rows — no error, just no change.
      - **`auth.role()` is deprecated — use the `TO` clause instead.** Supabase has deprecated `auth.role()` in favour of specifying the target role directly on the policy with `TO authenticated` or `TO anon`. Beyond deprecation, `auth.role() = 'authenticated'` breaks silently when anonymous sign-ins are enabled, because anonymous users carry the `authenticated` Postgres role and pass the check regardless of whether the user is genuinely signed in.
        ```sql
        -- Deprecated (do not use)
        create policy "example" on table_name for select
        using ( auth.role() = 'authenticated' );
        ```
      - **`TO authenticated` alone is authentication without authorization (BOLA / IDOR).** Using `TO authenticated` only checks the role — it does not restrict which rows a user can access. The correct pattern combines `TO authenticated` with an ownership predicate in `USING`:
        ```sql
        create policy "example" on table_name for select
        to authenticated
        using ( (select auth.uid()) = user_id );
        ```
      - **UPDATE policies require both `USING` and `WITH CHECK`.** Without `WITH CHECK`, a user can reassign a row's `user_id` to another user:
        ```sql
        create policy "example" on table_name for update
        to authenticated
        using ( (select auth.uid()) = user_id )
        with check ( (select auth.uid()) = user_id );
        ```
      - **`SECURITY DEFINER` functions bypass RLS.** A `SECURITY DEFINER` function runs with its creator's privileges — typically a role with `bypassrls` (e.g., `postgres`). Never add `SECURITY DEFINER` to resolve a permission error; it silently removes access control without fixing the underlying cause. Prefer `SECURITY INVOKER`.
      - **`SECURITY DEFINER` functions in `public` are callable by all roles.** Postgres grants `EXECUTE` to `PUBLIC` by default for every new function, so any `SECURITY DEFINER` function in `public` is a public API endpoint callable by `anon` and `authenticated` (which inherit from `PUBLIC`) without any additional grant. When `SECURITY DEFINER` is genuinely needed (e.g., bypassing RLS on an internal lookup table), keep the function in a non-exposed schema, always include an `auth.uid()` check in the function body, and run `supabase db advisors` after making changes.
    
    - **Storage access control**
      - **Storage upsert requires INSERT + SELECT + UPDATE.** Granting only INSERT allows new uploads but file replacement (upsert) silently fails. You need all three.
    
    - **Dependency and supply-chain security**
      - **Always pin package versions and commit lockfiles** when installing Supabase packages (`supabase-js`, `@supabase/ssr`, `supabase-py`, etc.). See the [npm security guide](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/security/npm-security.md) for the full checklist.
    
    For any security concern not covered above, fetch the Supabase product security index: `https://supabase.com/docs/guides/security/product-security.md`
    
    ## Supabase CLI
    
    Always discover commands via `--help` — never guess. The CLI structure changes between versions.
    
    ```bash
    supabase --help                    # All top-level commands
    supabase <group> --help            # Subcommands (e.g., supabase db --help)
    supabase <group> <command> --help  # Flags for a specific command
    ```
    
    **Supabase CLI Known gotchas:**
    
    - `supabase db query` requires **CLI v2.79.0+** → use MCP `execute_sql` or `psql` as fallback
    - `supabase db advisors` requires **CLI v2.81.3+** → use MCP `get_advisors` as fallback
    - The browser flow of `supabase login` creates a classic token with full account access. In CI, set `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN` to a scoped personal access token instead. It takes precedence over any saved login. Commands that connect with the database password (`--password` or `SUPABASE_DB_PASSWORD`, for example `supabase db push`) aren't limited by the token's permissions.
    - In imperative migration projects, create new hand-authored migration files with `supabase migration new <name>` first. Never invent a migration filename or rely on memory for the expected format. Declarative schema projects generate migrations from `supabase/schemas/`; see "Making and Committing Schema Changes" below.
    
    **Version check and upgrade:** Run `supabase --version` to check. For CLI changelogs and version-specific features, consult the [CLI documentation](https://supabase.com/docs/reference/cli/introduction) or [GitHub releases](https://github.com/supabase/cli/releases).
    
    ## Supabase MCP Server
    
    For setup instructions, server URL, and configuration, see the [MCP setup guide](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/getting-started/mcp).
    
    **Troubleshooting connection issues** — follow these steps in order:
    
    1. **Check if the server is reachable:**
       `curl -so /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" https://mcp.supabase.com/mcp`
       A `401` is expected (no token) and means the server is up. Timeout or "connection refused" means it may be down.
    
    2. **Check `.mcp.json` configuration:**
       Verify the project root has a valid `.mcp.json` with the correct server URL. If missing, create one pointing to `https://mcp.supabase.com/mcp`.
    
    3. **Authenticate the MCP server:**
       If the server is reachable and `.mcp.json` is correct but tools aren't visible, the user needs to authenticate. The Supabase MCP server uses OAuth 2.1 — tell the user to trigger the auth flow in their agent, complete it in the browser, and reload the session.
       In CI or other environments where the browser flow can't run, pass a scoped personal access token in the `Authorization: Bearer` header instead.
    
    4. **Check scoped token permissions:**
       If a tool fails with "You do not have permission to perform this action" while using a scoped personal access token, the token lacks that tool's permission or the project is outside the token's scope. Check the tool's required permission in the [personal access tokens guide](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/personal-access-tokens.md#mcp-tools).
    
    ## Supabase Documentation
    
    Before implementing any Supabase feature, find the relevant documentation. Use these methods in priority order:
    
    1. **MCP `search_docs` tool** (preferred — returns relevant snippets directly)
    2. **Fetch docs pages as markdown** — any docs page can be fetched by appending `.md` to the URL path.
    3. **Web search** for Supabase-specific topics when you don't know which page to look at.
    
    ## Making and Committing Schema Changes
    
    First decide which schema workflow the project uses.
    
    ### Option A: Declarative schemas
    
    Use this when `supabase/schemas/` exists or `config.toml` sets `schema_paths`. Edit the desired schema state in those files, then generate and review the migration. Do not start by hand-writing a migration. See the [Declarative database schemas guide](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/local-development/declarative-database-schemas).
    
    ### Option B: Imperative migrations
    
    Use this when the project does not use declarative schemas.
    
    **To make schema changes, use `execute_sql` (MCP) or `supabase db query` (CLI).** These run SQL directly on the database without creating migration history entries, so you can iterate freely and generate a clean migration when ready.
    
    Do NOT use `apply_migration` to change a local database schema — it writes a migration history entry on every call, which means you can't iterate, and `supabase db diff` / `supabase db pull` will produce empty or conflicting diffs. If you use it, you'll be stuck with whatever SQL you passed on the first try.
    
    **When ready to commit** your changes to a migration file:
    
    1. **Run advisors** → `supabase db advisors` (CLI v2.81.3+) or MCP `get_advisors`. Fix any issues.
    2. **Review the Security Checklist above** if your changes involve views, functions, triggers, or storage.
    3. **Generate the migration** → `supabase db pull <descriptive-name> --local --yes`
    4. **Verify** → `supabase migration list --local`
    
    ## Debugging
    
    When you get an error on a Supabase-related request, for example an error code from the Supabase REST API, Postgres database, or PostgREST, an empty result, getting blocked by RLS unexpectedly, or an error from a Supabase service like Auth, Realtime, Edge Functions, or Storage, you **must** fetch Supabase's [Monitoring and Debugging](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/monitoring-and-debugging.md) documentation before diagnosing or proposing a fix, rather than working from memory. The same docs also cover performance optimizations, such as slow queries and missing indexes.
    
    ## Reference Guides
    
    - **Skill Feedback** → [references/skill-feedback.md](references/skill-feedback.md)
      **MUST read when** the user reports that this skill gave incorrect guidance or is missing information.
    ✓ • 0ms

    ---
    name: supabase-postgres-best-practices
    description: "Postgres best practices maintained by Supabase, for Postgres running anywhere. Load this skill BEFORE writing or changing anything that lives in a Postgres database: creating or altering tables and columns (including choosing column types), schema design, migrations and declarative schema files, RLS policies and the tests that verify them, indexes, triggers, database functions, queues and scheduled jobs (pg_cron, pgmq), vector/semantic search (pgvector), and restoring dumps (pg_restore) or importing data. Also load it when diagnosing slow queries, high CPU, timeouts, EXPLAIN plans, connection exhaustion, locking, bloat, or rows visible to the wrong user or tenant. This is not just a performance guide — schema, migration, security, and SQL authoring tasks need these rules too, even for a one-column change or a single query."
    license: MIT
    metadata:
      author: supabase
      version: "1.1.1"
      organization: Supabase
      date: January 2026
      abstract: Comprehensive Postgres performance optimization guide for developers using Supabase and Postgres. Contains performance rules across 8 categories, prioritized by impact from critical (query performance, connection management) to incremental (advanced features). Each rule includes detailed explanations, incorrect vs. correct SQL examples, query plan analysis, and specific performance metrics to guide automated optimization and code generation.
    ---
    
    # Supabase Postgres Best Practices
    
    Comprehensive performance optimization guide for Postgres, maintained by Supabase. Contains rules across 8 categories, prioritized by impact to guide automated query optimization and schema design.
    
    ## When to Apply
    
    Reference these guidelines when:
    - Writing SQL queries or designing schemas
    - Implementing indexes or query optimization
    - Reviewing database performance issues
    - Configuring connection pooling or scaling
    - Optimizing for Postgres-specific features
    - Working with Row-Level Security (RLS)
    
    ## Rule Categories by Priority
    
    | Priority | Category | Impact | Prefix |
    |----------|----------|--------|--------|
    | 1 | Query Performance | CRITICAL | `query-` |
    | 2 | Connection Management | CRITICAL | `conn-` |
    | 3 | Security & RLS | CRITICAL | `security-` |
    | 4 | Schema Design | HIGH | `schema-` |
    | 5 | Concurrency & Locking | MEDIUM-HIGH | `lock-` |
    | 6 | Data Access Patterns | MEDIUM | `data-` |
    | 7 | Monitoring & Diagnostics | LOW-MEDIUM | `monitor-` |
    | 8 | Advanced Features | LOW | `advanced-` |
    
    ## How to Use
    
    Read individual rule files for detailed explanations and SQL examples:
    
    ```
    references/query-missing-indexes.md
    references/query-partial-indexes.md
    references/_sections.md
    ```
    
    Each rule file contains:
    - Brief explanation of why it matters
    - Incorrect SQL example with explanation
    - Correct SQL example with explanation
    - Optional EXPLAIN output or metrics
    - Additional context and references
    - Supabase-specific notes (when applicable)
    
    ## References
    
    - https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/
    - https://supabase.com/docs
    - https://wiki.postgresql.org/wiki/Performance_Optimization
    - https://supabase.com/docs/guides/database/overview
    - https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/row-level-security

    # Supabase Changelog
    
    ## Upgrade Postgres to fix local time for Vancouver, Edmonton, and Casablanca
    
    2026-10-30 · Bug Fix · Database · [supabase.com/changelog/tzdata-local-time-vancouver-edmonton-casablanca](https://supabase.com/changelog/tzdata-local-time-vancouver-edmonton-casablanca)
    
    Older Supabase Postgres versions return wrong local times for America/Vancouver, America/Edmonton, and Africa/Casablanca. Upgrade to 15.19.0.003 or 17.11.0.003 or later to fix it.
    
    ---
    --
    2026-10-14 · New Feature · supabase-dotnet · [supabase.com/changelog/dotnet-opentelemetry-support](https://supabase.com/changelog/dotnet-opentelemetry-support)
    
    The .NET SDK now emits traces and metrics for Auth, Postgrest, Storage, and Functions. Collect them with OpenTelemetry and export to any observability backend.
    
    ---
    --
    ## An outdated subset of TLS 1.2 ciphers will be disabled on project HTTP APIs
    
    2026-10-12 · Breaking Change · Auth, Data APIs, Storage, Realtime, Edge Functions · [supabase.com/changelog/cbc-tls-ciphers-deprecated](https://supabase.com/changelog/cbc-tls-ciphers-deprecated)
    
    Supabase will be disabling a subset of TLS 1.2 ciphers on project HTTP APIs on November 10, 2026. Clients that don't support modern standards (<0.0001% of Supabase traffic) will fail to connect.
    --
    ## Scoped personal access tokens are now generally available
    
    2026-10-06 · New Feature · Platform, Security, CLI, AI · [supabase.com/changelog/scoped-personal-access-tokens-ga](https://supabase.com/changelog/scoped-personal-access-tokens-ga)
    
    Scoped personal access tokens are now available to everyone. Limit a token to specific projects or organizations and grant only the permissions it needs on the Management API, MCP server, and CLI.
    --
    ## Organization invites support the No access role
    
    2026-10-05 · Improvement · Platform, Studio, Security · [supabase.com/changelog/no-access-org-role](https://supabase.com/changelog/no-access-org-role)
    
    Organization invites on Team and Enterprise plans now support the No access role, so admins can default new members to zero project access instead of Read-only access to every project.
    --
    2026-10-05 · New Feature · supabase-go · [supabase.com/changelog/supabase-go-public-alpha](https://supabase.com/changelog/supabase-go-public-alpha)
    
    The official Supabase Go SDK v0.1.0-alpha.1 is in public alpha. Its initial feature set lets your backend verify end-user access tokens and query your database as that user under Row Level Security.
    
    ---
    --
    ---
    
    ## [Private Alpha] Multigres: operating system for Postgres
    
    2026-10-01 · New Feature · Database, Multigres · [supabase.com/changelog/multigres-private-alpha](https://supabase.com/changelog/multigres-private-alpha)
    --
    2026-10-01 · Policy · Database, Studio, CLI, Auth, Storage, Realtime, Edge Functions · [supabase.com/changelog/orioledb-public-beta](https://supabase.com/changelog/orioledb-public-beta)
    
    OrioleDB is now in Public Beta. Create OrioleDB projects on Free, Pro, Team, and Enterprise plans, with paid features and standard Postgres pricing. Existing alpha projects don't change.
    
    ---
    --
    ---
    
    ## PostgreSQL 15.19 / 17.11 minor release — action may be required for ltree, pgcrypto, btree_gist, and custom operators
    
    2026-09-25 · Breaking Change · Database · [supabase.com/changelog/postgres-15-19-17-11-breaking-changes](https://supabase.com/changelog/postgres-15-19-17-11-breaking-changes)
    
    Postgres minor upgrade to 15.19/17.11 fixes 44 CVEs.
    ltree and btree_gist indexes may need reindexing, and pgcrypto data encrypted with legacy ciphers needs re-encryption.
    
    --
    ## Migration of Supabase Management API `logs.all` analytics endpoint to `logs` endpoint
    
    2026-07-23 · Breaking Change · Platform · [supabase.com/changelog/48235-migration-of-supabase-management-api-logs-all-analytics-endpoint-to-logs-endpoint](https://supabase.com/changelog/48235-migration-of-supabase-management-api-logs-all-analytics-endpoint-to-logs-endpoint)
    
    The Supabase Management API `logs.all` endpoint is removed on 2026-09-23. Scripts calling it must migrate to the new ClickHouse-backed `logs` endpoint, which accepts ClickHouse SQL only.
    --
    ## Extension version pinning is deprecated in favor of default versions
    
    2026-07-22 · Breaking Change · Database, Security · [supabase.com/changelog/extension-version-pinning-ignored](https://supabase.com/changelog/extension-version-pinning-ignored)
    
    Specifying an explicit version in CREATE or ALTER EXTENSION is deprecated. From 2026-08-05 the version clause is ignored and the extension's default version is installed, with a warning.
    --
    2026-07-21 · New Feature · ETL · [supabase.com/changelog/pipelines](https://supabase.com/changelog/pipelines)
    
    Stream Postgres changes to BigQuery in near real time with Supabase Pipelines, a managed CDC service configured right in the Dashboard. Now in public alpha on all paid plans.

        import hashlib, shutil
        src=Path('/home/kiri_/projects/adif-web')
        dst=src/'.worktrees/codex-recuperacion-persistencia'
        files=list((src/'app/api/practice-sessions').rglob('*.ts'))+[src/'lib/content/practice-session-types.ts',src/'supabase/migrations/20261103000000_practice_sessions.sql']

    -- Migration: practice_sessions and practice_session_answers
    -- Date: 2026-11-02
    -- Purpose: Secure persistence of practice sessions with RLS and idempotency
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 1. practice_sessions table
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE TABLE public.practice_sessions (
      id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
      user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      domain TEXT NOT NULL,
      topic_id TEXT,
      mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (mode IN ('training', 'practice')),
      question_count INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (question_count > 0),
      question_ids TEXT[] NOT NULL,
      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CREATED' CHECK (status IN ('CREATED', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'SUBMITTED', 'COMPLETED', 'ABANDONED')),
      version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
      started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
      completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
      total_elapsed_ms INTEGER DEFAULT 0 CHECK (total_elapsed_ms >= 0),
      client_event_id UUID NOT NULL,
      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
      updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
    );
    
    -- Composite unique for FK from answers
    ALTER TABLE public.practice_sessions ADD CONSTRAINT uq_practice_sessions_id_user UNIQUE (id, user_id);
    
    -- Indexes
    CREATE INDEX idx_practice_sessions_user ON public.practice_sessions(user_id);
    CREATE INDEX idx_practice_sessions_status ON public.practice_sessions(status);
    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_practice_sessions_client_event ON public.practice_sessions(client_event_id);
    
    -- RLS
    ALTER TABLE public.practice_sessions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
    
    CREATE POLICY "practice_sessions_select_own"
      ON public.practice_sessions FOR SELECT
      USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
    
    CREATE POLICY "practice_sessions_insert_own"
      ON public.practice_sessions FOR INSERT
      WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
    
    -- No direct UPDATE/DELETE from client (only via RPC)
    -- (No policies for UPDATE/DELETE = denied by default with RLS enabled)
    
    -- Permissions: anon has no access, authenticated can SELECT/INSERT only
    REVOKE ALL ON public.practice_sessions FROM anon;
    GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON public.practice_sessions TO authenticated;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 2. practice_session_answers table
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE TABLE public.practice_session_answers (
      id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
      session_id UUID NOT NULL,
      user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      question_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      selected_answer TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (selected_answer IN ('A', 'B', 'C', 'D')),
      elapsed_ms INTEGER CHECK (elapsed_ms >= 0),
      client_event_id UUID NOT NULL,
      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
      updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
      -- Composite FK: ensures session_id and user_id belong together
      FOREIGN KEY (session_id, user_id) REFERENCES public.practice_sessions(id, user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      UNIQUE(session_id, question_id)
    );
    
    -- Indexes
    CREATE INDEX idx_session_answers_session ON public.practice_session_answers(session_id);
    CREATE INDEX idx_session_answers_user ON public.practice_session_answers(user_id);
    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_session_answers_client_event ON public.practice_session_answers(client_event_id);
    
    -- RLS
    ALTER TABLE public.practice_session_answers ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
    
    CREATE POLICY "practice_session_answers_select_own"
      ON public.practice_session_answers FOR SELECT
      USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
    
    -- No direct INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE from client (only via RPC)
    -- (No policies for INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE = denied by default with RLS enabled)
    
    -- Permissions: anon has no access, authenticated can SELECT only
    REVOKE ALL ON public.practice_session_answers FROM anon;
    GRANT SELECT ON public.practice_session_answers TO authenticated;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 3. Idempotency log (persistent)
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE TABLE public.practice_session_events (
      id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
      user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      client_event_id UUID NOT NULL,
      operation TEXT NOT NULL,
      payload_fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL,
      result JSONB,
      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
      UNIQUE(user_id, client_event_id)
    );
    
    ALTER TABLE public.practice_session_events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
    
    -- No client access at all (only via RPC with SECURITY DEFINER)
    REVOKE ALL ON public.practice_session_events FROM anon, authenticated;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 4. RPC: create_practice_session
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.create_practice_session(
      p_domain TEXT,
      p_topic_id TEXT,
      p_mode TEXT,
      p_question_ids TEXT[],
      p_client_event_id UUID
    )
    RETURNS JSONB
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'private'
    AS $function$
    declare
      v_user_id uuid := auth.uid();
      v_session public.practice_sessions%rowtype;
      v_fingerprint text;
      v_event public.practice_session_events%rowtype;
    begin
      if v_user_id is null then
        raise exception 'Authentication is required.' using errcode = '28000';
      end if;
      if p_client_event_id is null then
        raise exception 'Idempotency key is required.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
      if p_domain is null or p_domain = '' then
        raise exception 'Domain is required.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
      if p_mode not in ('training', 'practice') then
        raise exception 'Invalid mode.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
      if p_question_ids is null or array_length(p_question_ids, 1) is null or array_length(p_question_ids, 1) = 0 then
        raise exception 'Question IDs are required.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      v_fingerprint := md5(jsonb_build_array(p_domain, p_topic_id, p_mode, p_question_ids)::text);
    
      -- Check idempotency
      select * into v_event
      from public.practice_session_events
      where user_id = v_user_id and client_event_id = p_client_event_id;
    
      if found then
        if v_event.payload_fingerprint is distinct from v_fingerprint then
          raise exception 'Idempotency key was already used with a different payload.' using errcode = '23514';
        end if;
        -- Return existing session
        select * into v_session from public.practice_sessions where client_event_id = p_client_event_id and user_id = v_user_id;
        return jsonb_build_object('sessionId', v_session.id, 'status', v_session.status, 'created', false);
      end if;
    
      -- Create session
      insert into public.practice_sessions (user_id, domain, topic_id, mode, question_count, question_ids, client_event_id)
      values (v_user_id, p_domain, p_topic_id, p_mode, array_length(p_question_ids, 1), p_question_ids, p_client_event_id)
      returning * into v_session;
    
      -- Record event
      insert into public.practice_session_events (user_id, client_event_id, operation, payload_fingerprint, result)
      values (v_user_id, p_client_event_id, 'create_session', v_fingerprint, jsonb_build_object('sessionId', v_session.id));
    
      return jsonb_build_object('sessionId', v_session.id, 'status', v_session.status, 'created', true);
    end;
    $function$;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 5. RPC: get_practice_session
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_practice_session(p_session_id UUID)
    RETURNS JSONB
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public'
    AS $function$
    declare
      v_user_id uuid := auth.uid();
      v_session public.practice_sessions%rowtype;
      v_answers jsonb;
    begin
      if v_user_id is null then
        raise exception 'Authentication is required.' using errcode = '28000';
      end if;
    
      select * into v_session
      from public.practice_sessions
      where id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id;
    
      if not found then
        raise exception 'Session not found.' using errcode = 'P0002';
      end if;
    
      select coalesce(jsonb_object_agg(question_id, selected_answer), '{}'::jsonb) into v_answers
      from public.practice_session_answers
      where session_id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id;
    
      return jsonb_build_object(
        'id', v_session.id,
        'domain', v_session.domain,
        'topicId', v_session.topic_id,
        'mode', v_session.mode,
        'questionCount', v_session.question_count,
        'questionIds', v_session.question_ids,
        'status', v_session.status,
        'version', v_session.version,
        'startedAt', v_session.started_at,
        'completedAt', v_session.completed_at,
        'totalElapsedMs', v_session.total_elapsed_ms,
        'answers', v_answers
      );
    end;
    $function$;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 6. RPC: save_practice_answer
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.save_practice_answer(
      p_session_id UUID,
      p_question_id TEXT,
      p_selected_answer TEXT,
      p_elapsed_ms INTEGER,
      p_client_event_id UUID,
      p_expected_version INTEGER
    )
    RETURNS JSONB
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public'
    AS $function$
    declare
      v_user_id uuid := auth.uid();
      v_session public.practice_sessions%rowtype;
      v_fingerprint text;
      v_event public.practice_session_events%rowtype;
      v_answer public.practice_session_answers%rowtype;
    begin
      if v_user_id is null then
        raise exception 'Authentication is required.' using errcode = '28000';
      end if;
      if p_client_event_id is null then
        raise exception 'Idempotency key is required.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
      if p_selected_answer not in ('A', 'B', 'C', 'D') then
        raise exception 'Invalid answer.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
      if p_elapsed_ms is not null and (p_elapsed_ms < 0 or p_elapsed_ms > 86400000) then
        raise exception 'Elapsed time outside range.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      -- Lock session row for update
      select * into v_session
      from public.practice_sessions
      where id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id
      for update;
    
      if not found then
        raise exception 'Session not found.' using errcode = 'P0002';
      end if;
    
      -- Check session not finalized
      if v_session.status in ('COMPLETED', 'ABANDONED', 'SUBMITTED') then
        raise exception 'Session is finalized.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      -- Optimistic concurrency check
      if p_expected_version is not null and v_session.version is distinct from p_expected_version then
        raise exception 'Version conflict.' using errcode = '40001';
      end if;
    
      -- Validate question is in session
      if not (p_question_id = ANY(v_session.question_ids)) then
        raise exception 'Question not in session.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      v_fingerprint := md5(jsonb_build_array(p_session_id, p_question_id, p_selected_answer, p_elapsed_ms)::text);
    
      -- Check idempotency
      select * into v_event
      from public.practice_session_events
      where user_id = v_user_id and client_event_id = p_client_event_id;
    
      if found then
        if v_event.payload_fingerprint is distinct from v_fingerprint then
          raise exception 'Idempotency key was already used with a different payload.' using errcode = '23514';
        end if;
        return jsonb_build_object('saved', true, 'idempotent', true, 'version', v_session.version);
      end if;
    
      -- Upsert answer
      insert into public.practice_session_answers (session_id, user_id, question_id, selected_answer, elapsed_ms, client_event_id)
      values (v_session.id, v_user_id, p_question_id, p_selected_answer, p_elapsed_ms, p_client_event_id)
      on conflict (session_id, question_id) do update
      set selected_answer = excluded.selected_answer,
          elapsed_ms = excluded.elapsed_ms,
          updated_at = now()
      returning * into v_answer;
    
      -- Update session status and version
      update public.practice_sessions
      set status = 'IN_PROGRESS',
          version = version + 1,
          updated_at = now()
      where id = v_session.id;
    
      -- Record event
      insert into public.practice_session_events (user_id, client_event_id, operation, payload_fingerprint, result)
      values (v_user_id, p_client_event_id, 'save_answer', v_fingerprint, jsonb_build_object('answerId', v_answer.id));
    
      return jsonb_build_object('saved', true, 'idempotent', false, 'version', v_session.version + 1);
    end;
    $function$;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 7. RPC: submit_practice_session (atomic finalization)
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.submit_practice_session(
      p_session_id UUID,
      p_client_event_id UUID
    )
    RETURNS JSONB
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'private'
    AS $function$
    declare
      v_user_id uuid := auth.uid();
      v_session public.practice_sessions%rowtype;
      v_answer record;
      v_question record;
      v_is_correct boolean;
      v_score_correct integer := 0;
      v_score_total integer := 0;
      v_results jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
      v_fingerprint text;
      v_event public.practice_session_events%rowtype;
      v_elapsed_sum integer := 0;
    begin
      if v_user_id is null then
        raise exception 'Authentication is required.' using errcode = '28000';
      end if;
      if p_client_event_id is null then
        raise exception 'Idempotency key is required.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      v_fingerprint := md5(jsonb_build_array(p_session_id)::text);
    
      -- Check idempotency
      select * into v_event
      from public.practice_session_events
      where user_id = v_user_id and client_event_id = p_client_event_id;
    
      if found then
        if v_event.payload_fingerprint is distinct from v_fingerprint then
          raise exception 'Idempotency key was already used with a different payload.' using errcode = '23514';
        end if;
        -- Return existing result
        select * into v_session from public.practice_sessions where id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id;
        return jsonb_build_object('status', v_session.status, 'idempotent', true);
      end if;
    
      -- Lock session
      select * into v_session
      from public.practice_sessions
      where id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id
      for update;
    
      if not found then
        raise exception 'Session not found.' using errcode = 'P0002';
      end if;
    
      -- Check if already completed
      if v_session.status = 'COMPLETED' then
        return jsonb_build_object('status', 'COMPLETED', 'idempotent', true);
      end if;
    
      if v_session.status not in ('CREATED', 'IN_PROGRESS') then
        raise exception 'Session cannot be submitted in current state.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      -- Process each answer
      for v_answer in
        select * from public.practice_session_answers
        where session_id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id
      loop
        -- Get question from authorized bank (via private schema or inline)
        select * into v_question
        from private.active_learning_questions
        where question_id = v_answer.question_id;
    
        -- If not found in learning_questions, try original practice
        if not found then
          -- For original practice questions, we'll handle separately
          -- For now, skip if not found
          continue;
        end if;
    
        v_is_correct := v_answer.selected_answer = v_question.correct_answer;
        if v_is_correct then v_score_correct := v_score_correct + 1; end if;
        v_score_total := v_score_total + 1;
        v_elapsed_sum := v_elapsed_sum + coalesce(v_answer.elapsed_ms, 0);
    
        v_results := v_results || jsonb_build_object(
          v_answer.question_id,
          jsonb_build_object('isCorrect', v_is_correct, 'selectedAnswer', v_answer.selected_answer)
        );
      end loop;
    
      -- Update session to COMPLETED
      update public.practice_sessions
      set status = 'COMPLETED',
          completed_at = now(),
          total_elapsed_ms = v_elapsed_sum,
          version = version + 1,
          updated_at = now()
      where id = v_session.id;
    
      -- Record event
      insert into public.practice_session_events (user_id, client_event_id, operation, payload_fingerprint, result)
      values (v_user_id, p_client_event_id, 'submit_session', v_fingerprint,
        jsonb_build_object('scoreCorrect', v_score_correct, 'scoreTotal', v_score_total));
    
      return jsonb_build_object(
        'status', 'COMPLETED',
        'scoreCorrect', v_score_correct,
        'scoreTotal', v_score_total,
        'results', v_results,
        'idempotent', false
      );
    end;
    $function$;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 8. RPC: abandon_practice_session
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.abandon_practice_session(
      p_session_id UUID,
      p_client_event_id UUID
    )
    RETURNS JSONB
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public'
    AS $function$
    declare
      v_user_id uuid := auth.uid();
      v_session public.practice_sessions%rowtype;
    begin
      if v_user_id is null then
        raise exception 'Authentication is required.' using errcode = '28000';
      end if;
    
      select * into v_session
      from public.practice_sessions
      where id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id
      for update;
    
      if not found then
        raise exception 'Session not found.' using errcode = 'P0002';
      end if;
    
      if v_session.status in ('COMPLETED', 'ABANDONED') then
        return jsonb_build_object('status', v_session.status, 'idempotent', true);
      end if;
    
      update public.practice_sessions
      set status = 'ABANDONED',
          version = version + 1,
          updated_at = now()
      where id = v_session.id;
    
      return jsonb_build_object('status', 'ABANDONED', 'idempotent', false);
    end;
    $function$;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 9. Grants for RPC functions
    -- ============================================================
    GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.create_practice_session TO authenticated;
    GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_practice_session TO authenticated;
    GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.save_practice_answer TO authenticated;
    GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.submit_practice_session TO authenticated;
    GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.abandon_practice_session TO authenticated;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 10. Updated_at trigger
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_updated_at()
    RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
    BEGIN
      NEW.updated_at = now();
      RETURN NEW;
    END;
    $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
    
    CREATE TRIGGER trg_practice_sessions_updated_at
      BEFORE UPDATE ON public.practice_sessions
      FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.set_updated_at();
    
    CREATE TRIGGER trg_practice_session_answers_updated_at
      BEFORE UPDATE ON public.practice_session_answers
      FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.set_updated_at();
    MD
    echo "done"
    ✓ • 24ms

    # For detailed configuration reference documentation, visit:
    # https://supabase.com/docs/guides/local-development/cli/config
    # A string used to distinguish different Supabase projects on the same host. Defaults to the
    # working directory name when running `supabase init`.
    project_id = "adif-web"
    
    [api]
    enabled = true
    # Port to use for the API URL.
    port = 54321
    # Schemas to expose in your API. Tables, views and stored procedures in this schema will get API
    # endpoints. `public` and `graphql_public` schemas are included by default.
    schemas = ["public", "graphql_public"]
    # Extra schemas to add to the search_path of every request.
    extra_search_path = ["public", "extensions"]
    # The maximum number of rows returns from a view, table, or stored procedure. Limits payload size
    # for accidental or malicious requests.
    max_rows = 1000
    # Controls whether new tables, views, sequences and functions created in the `public` schema by
    # `postgres` are reachable through the Data API roles (`anon`, `authenticated`, `service_role`)
    # without explicit GRANTs, matching the cloud default. Set to `false` to require explicit GRANTs
    # instead. Left unset, a fresh project falls back to `true`.
    # auto_expose_new_tables = true
    
    [api.tls]
    # Enable HTTPS endpoints locally using a self-signed certificate.
    enabled = false
    # Paths to self-signed certificate pair.
    # cert_path = "../certs/my-cert.pem"
    # key_path = "../certs/my-key.pem"
    
    [db]
    # Port to use for the local database URL.
    port = 54322
    # Port used by db diff command to initialize the shadow database.
    shadow_port = 54320
    # Maximum amount of time to wait for health check when starting the local database.
    health_timeout = "2m"
    # The database major version to use. This has to be the same as your remote database's. Run `SHOW
    # server_version;` on the remote database to check.
    major_version = 17
    # OrioleDB version to use as the Postgres storage engine. Leave empty to use the default engine.
    orioledb_version = ""
    
    [db.pooler]
    enabled = false
    # Port to use for the local connection pooler.
    port = 54329
    # Specifies when a server connection can be reused by other clients.
    # Configure one of the supported pooler modes: `transaction`, `session`.
    pool_mode = "transaction"
    # How many server connections to allow per user/database pair.

    create view private.active_learning_questions as
    select question.*
    from private.learning_questions question
    where question.active
      and not exists (
        select 1
        from unnest(question.concept_ids) as mapped(concept_id)
        left join private.learning_concepts concept on concept.concept_id = mapped.concept_id
        where concept.concept_id is null or not concept.active
      );
    
    revoke all on table private.active_learning_questions from public, anon, authenticated;
    
    alter table public.question_attempts add column client_event_id uuid;
    alter table public.question_attempts add column request_fingerprint text;
    alter table public.question_attempts
      add constraint question_attempts_user_client_event_key unique (user_id, client_event_id);
    
    alter table public.simulation_attempts add column client_event_id uuid;
    alter table public.simulation_attempts add column request_fingerprint text;
    alter table public.simulation_attempts
      add constraint simulation_attempts_user_client_event_key unique (user_id, client_event_id);
    
    create or replace function private.derived_event_id(
      p_client_event_id uuid,
      p_question_id text,
      p_concept_id text
    )
    returns uuid
    language sql
    immutable
    strict
    set search_path = pg_catalog
    as $$
      select (
        substr(v_hash, 1, 8) || '-' || substr(v_hash, 9, 4) || '-' ||
        substr(v_hash, 13, 4) || '-' || substr(v_hash, 17, 4) || '-' ||
        substr(v_hash, 21, 12)
      )::uuid
      from (select md5(p_client_event_id::text || ':' || p_question_id || ':' || p_concept_id) as v_hash) hashed;
    $$;
    
    create or replace function private.record_review_evidence(
      p_user_id uuid,
      p_concept_id text,
      p_source_kind text,
      p_question_id text,
      p_rating smallint,
      p_client_event_id uuid,
      p_occurred_at timestamptz,
      p_today date
    )
    returns boolean
    language plpgsql
    security definer
    set search_path = pg_catalog, public, private

    -- ============================================================
    -- 6. RPC: save_practice_answer
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.save_practice_answer(
      p_session_id UUID,
      p_question_id TEXT,
      p_selected_answer TEXT,
      p_elapsed_ms INTEGER,
      p_client_event_id UUID,
      p_expected_version INTEGER
    )
    RETURNS JSONB
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public'
    AS $function$
    declare
      v_user_id uuid := auth.uid();
      v_session public.practice_sessions%rowtype;
      v_fingerprint text;
      v_event public.practice_session_events%rowtype;
      v_answer public.practice_session_answers%rowtype;
    begin
      if v_user_id is null then
        raise exception 'Authentication is required.' using errcode = '28000';
      end if;
      if p_client_event_id is null then
        raise exception 'Idempotency key is required.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
      if p_selected_answer not in ('A', 'B', 'C', 'D') then
        raise exception 'Invalid answer.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
      if p_elapsed_ms is not null and (p_elapsed_ms < 0 or p_elapsed_ms > 86400000) then
        raise exception 'Elapsed time outside range.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      -- Lock session row for update
      select * into v_session
      from public.practice_sessions
      where id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id
      for update;
    
      if not found then
        raise exception 'Session not found.' using errcode = 'P0002';
      end if;
    
      -- Check session not finalized
      if v_session.status in ('COMPLETED', 'ABANDONED', 'SUBMITTED') then
        raise exception 'Session is finalized.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      -- Optimistic concurrency check
      if p_expected_version is not null and v_session.version is distinct from p_expected_version then
        raise exception 'Version conflict.' using errcode = '40001';
      end if;
    
      -- Validate question is in session
      if not (p_question_id = ANY(v_session.question_ids)) then
        raise exception 'Question not in session.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      v_fingerprint := md5(jsonb_build_array(p_session_id, p_question_id, p_selected_answer, p_elapsed_ms)::text);
    
      -- Check idempotency
      select * into v_event
      from public.practice_session_events
      where user_id = v_user_id and client_event_id = p_client_event_id;
    
      if found then
        if v_event.payload_fingerprint is distinct from v_fingerprint then
          raise exception 'Idempotency key was already used with a different payload.' using errcode = '23514';
        end if;
        return jsonb_build_object('saved', true, 'idempotent', true, 'version', v_session.version);
      end if;
    
      -- Upsert answer
      insert into public.practice_session_answers (session_id, user_id, question_id, selected_answer, elapsed_ms, client_event_id)
      values (v_session.id, v_user_id, p_question_id, p_selected_answer, p_elapsed_ms, p_client_event_id)
      on conflict (session_id, question_id) do update
      set selected_answer = excluded.selected_answer,
          elapsed_ms = excluded.elapsed_ms,
          updated_at = now()
      returning * into v_answer;
    
      -- Update session status and version
      update public.practice_sessions
      set status = 'IN_PROGRESS',
          version = version + 1,
          updated_at = now()
      where id = v_session.id;
    
      -- Record event
      insert into public.practice_session_events (user_id, client_event_id, operation, payload_fingerprint, result)
      values (v_user_id, p_client_event_id, 'save_answer', v_fingerprint, jsonb_build_object('answerId', v_answer.id));
    
      return jsonb_build_object('saved', true, 'idempotent', false, 'version', v_session.version + 1);
    end;
    $function$;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 7. RPC: submit_practice_session (atomic finalization)
    -- ============================================================
    CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.submit_practice_session(
      p_session_id UUID,
      p_client_event_id UUID
    )
    RETURNS JSONB
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'private'
    AS $function$
    declare
      v_user_id uuid := auth.uid();
      v_session public.practice_sessions%rowtype;
      v_answer record;
      v_question record;
      v_is_correct boolean;
      v_score_correct integer := 0;
      v_score_total integer := 0;
      v_results jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
      v_fingerprint text;
      v_event public.practice_session_events%rowtype;
      v_elapsed_sum integer := 0;
    begin
      if v_user_id is null then
        raise exception 'Authentication is required.' using errcode = '28000';
      end if;
      if p_client_event_id is null then
        raise exception 'Idempotency key is required.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      v_fingerprint := md5(jsonb_build_array(p_session_id)::text);
    
      -- Check idempotency
      select * into v_event
      from public.practice_session_events
      where user_id = v_user_id and client_event_id = p_client_event_id;
    
      if found then
        if v_event.payload_fingerprint is distinct from v_fingerprint then
          raise exception 'Idempotency key was already used with a different payload.' using errcode = '23514';
        end if;
        -- Return existing result
        select * into v_session from public.practice_sessions where id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id;
        return jsonb_build_object('status', v_session.status, 'idempotent', true);
      end if;
    
      -- Lock session
      select * into v_session
      from public.practice_sessions
      where id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id
      for update;
    
      if not found then
        raise exception 'Session not found.' using errcode = 'P0002';
      end if;
    
      -- Check if already completed
      if v_session.status = 'COMPLETED' then
        return jsonb_build_object('status', 'COMPLETED', 'idempotent', true);
      end if;
    
      if v_session.status not in ('CREATED', 'IN_PROGRESS') then
        raise exception 'Session cannot be submitted in current state.' using errcode = '23514';
      end if;
    
      -- Process each answer
      for v_answer in
        select * from public.practice_session_answers
        where session_id = p_session_id and user_id = v_user_id
      loop
        -- Get question from authorized bank (via private schema or inline)
        select * into v_question
        from private.active_learning_questions
        where question_id = v_answer.question_id;
    
        -- If not found in learning_questions, try original practice
        if not found then
          -- For original practice questions, we'll handle separately
          -- For now, skip if not found
          continue;
        end if;
    
        v_is_correct := v_answer.selected_answer = v_question.correct_answer;
        if v_is_correct then v_score_correct := v_score_correct + 1; end if;
        v_score_total := v_score_total + 1;
        v_elapsed_sum := v_elapsed_sum + coalesce(v_answer.elapsed_ms, 0);
    
        v_results := v_results || jsonb_build_object(
          v_answer.question_id,
          jsonb_build_object('isCorrect', v_is_correct, 'selectedAnswer', v_answer.selected_answer)
        );
      end loop;
    
      -- Update session to COMPLETED
      update public.practice_sessions
      set status = 'COMPLETED',
          completed_at = now(),
          total_elapsed_ms = v_elapsed_sum,
          version = version + 1,
          updated_at = now()
      where id = v_session.id;
    
      -- Record event
      insert into public.practice_session_events (user_id, client_event_id, operation, payload_fingerprint, result)
      values (v_user_id, p_client_event_id, 'submit_session', v_fingerprint,
        jsonb_build_object('scoreCorrect', v_score_correct, 'scoreTotal', v_score_total));
    
      return jsonb_build_object(
        'status', 'COMPLETED',
        'scoreCorrect', v_score_correct,
        'scoreTotal', v_score_total,
        'results', v_results,
        'idempotent', false
      );
    end;
    $function$;
    
    -- ============================================================
    -- 8. RPC: abandon_practice_session
    ✓ • 0ms

    \set ON_ERROR_STOP on
    
    begin;
    
    -- Transaction-local identities and reviewed-original-question fixtures. The
    -- rollback at the end leaves neither users nor registry rows in the database.
    insert into auth.users (id, email) values
      ('e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000001', 'practice-session-a@example.invalid'),
      ('e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000002', 'practice-session-b@example.invalid')
    on conflict (id) do nothing;
    
    insert into private.learning_concepts (concept_id, active)
    values ('test-practice-sessions', true)
    on conflict (concept_id) do update set active = true;
    
    insert into private.learning_questions (question_id, correct_answer, concept_ids, simulation_id, active)
    values
      ('orig-test-practice-sessions-correct', 'B', array['test-practice-sessions'], 'practice:original:test', true),
      ('orig-test-practice-sessions-second', 'D', array['test-practice-sessions'], 'practice:original:test', true)
    on conflict (question_id) do update
    set correct_answer = excluded.correct_answer,
        concept_ids = excluded.concept_ids,
        simulation_id = excluded.simulation_id,
        active = true;
    
    set local role authenticated;
    
    do $$
    declare
      v_user_a constant uuid := 'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000001';
      v_user_b constant uuid := 'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000002';
      v_question_a constant text := 'orig-test-practice-sessions-correct';
      v_question_b constant text := 'orig-test-practice-sessions-second';
      v_create jsonb;
      v_create_retry jsonb;
      v_save jsonb;
      v_save_retry jsonb;
      v_update jsonb;
      v_save_second jsonb;
      v_submit jsonb;
      v_submit_retry jsonb;
      v_session_a uuid;
      v_session_b uuid;
      v_rejected boolean;
    begin
      -- Create is idempotent for the same owner's key and payload.
      perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', v_user_a::text, true);
      v_create := public.create_practice_session(
        'general-knowledge', null, 'practice', array[v_question_a, v_question_b],
        'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000011'
      );
      v_session_a := (v_create->>'sessionId')::uuid;
      if v_create->>'created' is distinct from 'true' then
        raise exception 'First session create was not marked created.';
      end if;
    
      v_create_retry := public.create_practice_session(
        'general-knowledge', null, 'practice', array[v_question_a, v_question_b],
        'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000011'
      );
      if v_create_retry->>'sessionId' is distinct from v_session_a::text
         or v_create_retry->>'created' is distinct from 'false' then
        raise exception 'Same-payload create retry did not return the original session.';
      end if;
    
      v_rejected := false;
      begin
        perform public.create_practice_session(
          'telecom', null, 'practice', array[v_question_a],
          'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000011'
        );
      exception when check_violation then
        v_rejected := true;
      end;
      if not v_rejected then
        raise exception 'Create accepted a changed payload under an existing idempotency key.';
      end if;
    
      -- A's answer write advances the version once. Replaying the exact request
      -- with its original expected version must not create a conflict or a second
      -- answer/event.
      v_save := public.save_practice_answer(
        v_session_a, v_question_a, 'B', 1000,
        'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000012', 1
      );
      if (v_save->>'version')::integer <> 2 or v_save->>'idempotent' is distinct from 'false' then
        raise exception 'First answer save did not advance version 1 to 2.';
      end if;
    
      v_save_retry := public.save_practice_answer(
        v_session_a, v_question_a, 'B', 1000,
        'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000012', 1
      );
      if v_save_retry->>'idempotent' is distinct from 'true' then
        raise exception 'Same-payload answer retry was not marked idempotent.';
      end if;
    
      -- A new key may revise the answer, while a stale version with a different
      -- key must be rejected without changing the stored answer.
      v_update := public.save_practice_answer(
        v_session_a, v_question_a, 'A', 800,
        'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000013', 2
      );
      if (v_update->>'version')::integer <> 3 then
        raise exception 'Answer revision did not advance version 2 to 3.';
      end if;
    
      v_rejected := false;
      begin
        perform public.save_practice_answer(
          v_session_a, v_question_b, 'D', 700,
          'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000014', 2
        );
      exception when serialization_failure then
        v_rejected := true;
      end;
      if not v_rejected then
        raise exception 'Stale expected_version was accepted.';
      end if;
    
      v_save_second := public.save_practice_answer(
        v_session_a, v_question_b, 'D', 700,
        'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000015', 3
      );
      if (v_save_second->>'version')::integer <> 4 then
        raise exception 'Second answer save did not advance version 3 to 4.';
      end if;
    
      -- User B cannot list, load, or write A's session, but can create and see
      -- their own session with the same original question.
      perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', v_user_b::text, true);
      if (select count(*) from public.practice_sessions where id = v_session_a) <> 0
         or (select count(*) from public.practice_session_answers where session_id = v_session_a) <> 0 then
        raise exception 'User B can read User A session data through RLS.';
      end if;
    
      v_rejected := false;
      begin
        perform public.get_practice_session(v_session_a);
      exception when no_data_found then
        v_rejected := true;
      end;
      if not v_rejected then
        raise exception 'User B loaded User A session through the RPC.';
      end if;
    
      v_rejected := false;
      begin
        perform public.save_practice_answer(
          v_session_a, v_question_a, 'C', 50,
          'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000021', 4
        );
      exception when no_data_found then
        v_rejected := true;
      end;
      if not v_rejected then
        raise exception 'User B wrote an answer to User A session.';
      end if;
    
      v_create := public.create_practice_session(
        'general-knowledge', null, 'practice', array[v_question_a],
        'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000022'
      );
      v_session_b := (v_create->>'sessionId')::uuid;
      if (select count(*) from public.practice_sessions where id = v_session_b) <> 1 then
        raise exception 'User B cannot read their own session.';
      end if;
    
      -- Switch back to A and finalize. The two original questions have answers B
      -- (revised to wrong A) and D (correct), so server-side scoring must be 1/2.
      perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', v_user_a::text, true);
      if (select count(*) from public.practice_sessions where id = v_session_b) <> 0 then
        raise exception 'User A can read User B session through RLS.';
      end if;
    
      v_submit := public.submit_practice_session(
        v_session_a, 'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000016'
      );
      if v_submit->>'status' is distinct from 'COMPLETED'
         or v_submit->>'scoreCorrect' is distinct from '1'
         or v_submit->>'scoreTotal' is distinct from '2'
         or v_submit#>>array['results', v_question_a, 'isCorrect'] is distinct from 'false'
         or v_submit#>>array['results', v_question_a, 'selectedAnswer'] is distinct from 'A'
         or v_submit#>>array['results', v_question_b, 'isCorrect'] is distinct from 'true'
         or v_submit#>>array['results', v_question_b, 'selectedAnswer'] is distinct from 'D'
         or v_submit->>'idempotent' is distinct from 'false' then
        raise exception 'Original-question scoring or finalization result is incorrect: %', v_submit;
      end if;
    
      v_submit_retry := public.submit_practice_session(
        v_session_a, 'e1100000-0000-4e11-a000-000000000016'
      );
      if v_submit_retry->>'status' is distinct from 'COMPLETED'
         or v_submit_retry->>'idempotent' is distinct from 'true' then
        raise exception 'Same-key submit retry was not idempotent.';
      end if;
    end;
    $$;

    $ sed -n '1,180p' supabase/tests/practice_sessions_concurrent_same_event.sh
    #!/usr/bin/env bash
    set -Eeuo pipefail
    
    # Concurrent retry regression for save_practice_answer. This script is
    # deliberately pinned to the disposable adif-persistencia-codex container.
    # It creates and removes only randomly named fixtures; it does not alter the
    # database schema or read credentials from the host.
    
    readonly DB_CONTAINER="supabase_db_adif-persistencia-codex"
    readonly EXPECTED_COMPOSE_PROJECT="adif-persistencia-codex"
    readonly APP_HOLDER="practice-session-concurrency-holder"
    readonly APP_WRITER="practice-session-concurrency-writer"
    
    die() {
      printf 'ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
      exit 1
    }
    
    [[ "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" == "$0" ]] || die "Run this script directly, not by sourcing it."
    command -v docker >/dev/null || die "docker is required."
    command -v python3 >/dev/null || die "python3 is required to generate isolated fixture IDs."
    
    compose_project="$(docker inspect --format '{{ index .Config.Labels "com.docker.compose.project" }}' "$DB_CONTAINER" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    [[ "$compose_project" == "$EXPECTED_COMPOSE_PROJECT" ]] \
      || die "Refusing to use container '$DB_CONTAINER' with project label '$compose_project'."
    [[ "$(docker inspect --format '{{.State.Status}}' "$DB_CONTAINER")" == "running" ]] \
      || die "The isolated database container is not running."
    
    psql_db() {
      docker exec -i "$DB_CONTAINER" psql -X -qAt -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -U postgres -d postgres "$@"
    }
    
    schema_ready="$(psql_db -c "select (to_regclass('public.practice_sessions') is not null)::int || '|' || (to_regprocedure('public.save_practice_answer(uuid,text,text,integer,uuid,integer)') is not null)::int")"
    [[ "$schema_ready" == "1|1" ]] \
      || die "Practice-session migration is not present. Apply it only to the isolated test database before running this script."
    
    new_uuid() {
      python3 -c 'import uuid; print(uuid.uuid4())'
    }
    
    readonly fixture_user="$(new_uuid)"
    readonly fixture_session="$(new_uuid)"
    readonly fixture_create_event="$(new_uuid)"
    readonly fixture_write_event="$(new_uuid)"
    readonly fixture_question="concurrency-test-$(new_uuid)"
    readonly fixture_concept="concurrency-test-$(new_uuid)"
    readonly fixture_email="practice-concurrency-$(new_uuid)@example.invalid"
    readonly work_dir="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/practice-session-concurrency.XXXXXX")"
    
    holder_pid=""
    writer_pid=""
    
    cleanup() {
      local exit_code=$?
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      if [[ -n "$writer_pid" ]] && kill -0 "$writer_pid" 2>/dev/null; then
        kill "$writer_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        wait "$writer_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
      fi
      if [[ -n "$holder_pid" ]] && kill -0 "$holder_pid" 2>/dev/null; then
        kill "$holder_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        wait "$holder_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
      fi
      psql_db -c "
        delete from public.practice_session_events
        where user_id = '$fixture_user' and client_event_id in ('$fixture_create_event', '$fixture_write_event');
        delete from public.practice_sessions where id = '$fixture_session';
        delete from private.learning_questions where question_id = '$fixture_question';
        delete from private.learning_concepts where concept_id = '$fixture_concept';
        delete from auth.users where id = '$fixture_user';
      " >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
      rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
      exit "$exit_code"
    }
    trap cleanup EXIT
    trap 'exit 130' INT
    trap 'exit 143' TERM
    
    # Create disposable data as postgres. The answer is known only to this fixture;
    # both concurrent requests submit the same answer and idempotency key.
    psql_db <<SQL
    insert into auth.users (id, email)
    values ('$fixture_user', '$fixture_email');
    
    insert into private.learning_concepts (concept_id, active)
    values ('$fixture_concept', true);
    
    insert into private.learning_questions (question_id, correct_answer, concept_ids, simulation_id, active)
    values ('$fixture_question', 'B', array['$fixture_concept'], 'practice:original:concurrency-test', true);
    
    insert into public.practice_sessions
      (id, user_id, domain, mode, question_count, question_ids, status, version, client_event_id)
    values
      ('$fixture_session', '$fixture_user', 'general-knowledge', 'practice', 1,
       array['$fixture_question'], 'CREATED', 1, '$fixture_create_event');
    SQL
    
    # Connection A holds the session row lock before entering the save RPC. The
    # sleep creates a deterministic window for connection B to enter the same RPC
    # and block on that row. Both calls use expected_version=1 and the same key.
    docker exec -i -e PGAPPNAME="$APP_HOLDER" "$DB_CONTAINER" \
      psql -X -qAt -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v VERBOSITY=verbose -U postgres -d postgres \
      >"$work_dir/holder.out" 2>"$work_dir/holder.err" <<SQL &
    begin;
    select id from public.practice_sessions where id = '$fixture_session' for update;
    select pg_sleep(8);
    select set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', '$fixture_user', true);
    set local role authenticated;
    select public.save_practice_answer(
      '$fixture_session', '$fixture_question', 'B', 1000, '$fixture_write_event', 1
    );
    commit;
    SQL
    holder_pid=$!
    
    holder_ready=0
    for _ in $(seq 1 100); do
      holder_state="$(psql_db -c "select count(*) from pg_stat_activity where application_name = '$APP_HOLDER' and state = 'active' and wait_event = 'PgSleep'")"
      if [[ "$holder_state" == "1" ]]; then
        holder_ready=1
        break
      fi
      if ! kill -0 "$holder_pid" 2>/dev/null; then
        cat "$work_dir/holder.err" >&2
        die "The lock-holding connection exited before reaching its synchronization point."
      fi
      sleep 0.05
    done
    [[ "$holder_ready" == "1" ]] \
      || die "Could not confirm that connection A holds the row lock."
    
    # Connection B reaches save_practice_answer while A still owns the row lock.
    docker exec -i -e PGAPPNAME="$APP_WRITER" "$DB_CONTAINER" \
      psql -X -qAt -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -v VERBOSITY=verbose -U postgres -d postgres \
      >"$work_dir/writer.out" 2>"$work_dir/writer.err" <<SQL &
    begin;
    select set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', '$fixture_user', true);
    set local role authenticated;
    select public.save_practice_answer(
      '$fixture_session', '$fixture_question', 'B', 1000, '$fixture_write_event', 1
    );
    commit;
    SQL
    writer_pid=$!
    
    writer_blocked=0
    for _ in $(seq 1 60); do
      writer_state="$(psql_db -c "select count(*) from pg_stat_activity where application_name = '$APP_WRITER' and state = 'active' and wait_event_type = 'Lock'")"
      if [[ "$writer_state" == "1" ]]; then
        writer_blocked=1
        break
      fi
      if ! kill -0 "$writer_pid" 2>/dev/null; then
        break
      fi
      sleep 0.05
    done
    [[ "$writer_blocked" == "1" ]] \
      || die "Connection B did not block on A's row lock; concurrency was not established."
    
    set +e
    wait "$holder_pid"
    holder_status=$?
    holder_pid=""
    wait "$writer_pid"
    writer_status=$?
    writer_pid=""
    set -e
    
    printf 'Connection A exit=%s result=' "$holder_status"
    tail -n 1 "$work_dir/holder.out" || true
    printf 'Connection B exit=%s result=' "$writer_status"
    tail -n 1 "$work_dir/writer.out" || true
    if [[ "$holder_status" -ne 0 ]]; then cat "$work_dir/holder.err" >&2; fi
    if [[ "$writer_status" -ne 0 ]]; then cat "$work_dir/writer.err" >&2; fi
    
    state="$(psql_db -c "
      select s.status || '|' || s.version::text || '|' ||
             (select count(*)::text from public.practice_session_answers a where a.session_id = s.id) || '|' ||
             (select count(*)::text from public.practice_session_events e where e.user_id = s.user_id and e.client_event_id = '$fixture_write_event')
    ✓ • 34ms
